####################################################################################################
FILE: src\api\utils.py
####################################################################################################

import logging
import re
from typing import Literal

import httpx
from elasticsearch import AsyncElasticsearch

from dlh_ai.config import get_settings

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")
settings = get_settings()


async def get_elasticsearch_client() -> AsyncElasticsearch:
    """Create an Elasticsearch client."""
    return AsyncElasticsearch(
        hosts=settings.common.elasticsearch_hosts,
        api_key=settings.common.elasticsearch_api_key,
        verify_certs=settings.common.elasticsearch_verify_certs,
        # TODO: add other options as needed
    )


async def check_elasticsearch_availability() -> None:
    """Verify the Elasticsearch cluster is reachable before accepting requests."""
    logger.debug("checking Elasticsearch cluster availability")
    client = await get_elasticsearch_client()
    if not await client.info():
        raise RuntimeError("Elasticsearch cluster is not reachable")
    logger.info("Elasticsearch cluster is available")


async def check_embedding_service_health() -> None:
    """Verify the embedding service is reachable before accepting requests."""
    url = f"{settings.embedding_api.embedding_service_base_url}"
    logger.debug(f"checking embedding service health at {url}")
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        response = await client.get(
            url,
            timeout=10,
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
    logger.info("embedding service is available")


async def check_llm_service_health() -> None:
    """Verify the LLM service is reachable before accepting requests."""
    url = f"{settings.common.llm_base_url}"
    logger.debug(f"checking LLM service health at {url}")
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        response = await client.get(
            url,
            timeout=10,
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.common.llm_api_key}"},
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
    logger.info("LLM service is available")


def normalize_query(query: str) -> str:
    """Normalize a query string for consistent cache / search matching."""
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", query.strip().lower())


async def get_embedding_single_text(
    text: str, model: Literal["large", "small"] = "small"
) -> list[float]:
    """Generate an embedding for the text via the embedding service."""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        response = await client.post(
            f"{settings.embedding_api.embedding_service_base_url}/api/v1/embedding/{model}",
            json={"texts": [text]},
            # timeout=10, # REVIEW - Do we need to set a timeout?
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
    return response.json()["embeddings"][0]


async def get_embedding_multiple_texts(
    texts: list[str], model: Literal["large", "small"] = "small"
) -> list[list[float]]:
    """Generate embeddings for the texts via the embedding service."""
    async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
        response = await client.post(
            f"{settings.embedding_api.embedding_service_base_url}/api/v1/embedding/{model}",
            json={"texts": texts},
            # timeout=10, # REVIEW - Do we need to set a timeout?
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
    return response.json()["embeddings"]



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\api\embd_service\main.py
####################################################################################################

import asyncio
import http
import logging
import traceback
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from about_time import about_time
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, status
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from scalar_fastapi import get_scalar_api_reference

from dlh_ai.config import get_settings
from dlh_ai.models import EmbedddingResponse, EmbeddingRequest
from dlh_ai.utils import HDFSClient

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")
settings = get_settings()

SMALL_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH = settings.embedding_api.embedding_service_small_model_path
LARGE_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH = settings.embedding_api.embedding_service_large_model_path


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    hdfs_client = HDFSClient()

    logger.info(f"loading small embedding model from {SMALL_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH}")
    app.state.embedder_small = hdfs_client.load_model_sentence_transformer(
        SMALL_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH, backend="onnx"
    )

    logger.info(f"loading large embedding model from {LARGE_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH}")
    app.state.embedder_large = hdfs_client.load_model_sentence_transformer(
        LARGE_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH, backend="onnx"
    )

    yield
    # clean up the models after the lifespan is complete
    app.state.embedder_small = None
    app.state.embedder_large = None
    logger.info("cleaned up the models after the lifespan is complete")


app = FastAPI(
    debug=True,
    title="Yono Chatbot Embedding Service",
    version="0.0.1",
    docs_url=None,
    redoc_url=None,
    lifespan=lifespan,
)


async def _encode_texts(model, texts: list[str]) -> list[list[float]]:
    logger.debug(f"encoding {len(texts)} texts")
    # NOTE - Using asyncio.to_thread to encode the texts in a separate thread
    # to avoid blocking the main thread.
    embeddings = await asyncio.to_thread(model.encode, texts)
    return embeddings.tolist()


# Middleware to log incoming request & processing timing
@app.middleware("http")
async def _log_request_information(request: Request, call_next):
    # Reconstruct the path and query string without sensitive data
    path_and_query_url = (
        f"{request.url.path}?{request.query_params}"
        if request.query_params
        else request.url.path
    )

    # tracking time taken by request to be fulfilled
    with about_time() as t:  # type: ignore
        # NOTE - Here the actual http function will be executed in the middleware.
        logger.info(f"received request --> {request.method} {path_and_query_url}")
        response = await call_next(request)
        logger.info(
            f"fulfilled request --> {request.method} {path_and_query_url} {response.status_code}"
            f" {http.HTTPStatus(response.status_code).phrase} in {t.duration_human}"
        )
        return response


@app.exception_handler(Exception)
async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception):
    errors = traceback.format_exception(exc)
    logger.error("".join(errors))
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
        content={"error": exc.__class__.__name__, "detail": str(exc)},
    )


# Scalar interactive docs
@app.get("/docs", include_in_schema=False)
async def _internal_scalar_html():
    return get_scalar_api_reference(
        openapi_url=app.openapi_url,
        title=app.title,
    )


# REST API for health check
@app.get("/", status_code=200)
async def _index():
    """API Health check"""
    return {
        "message": "Yono Chatbot Embedding Service is up and running",
        "models": {
            "small": SMALL_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH,
            "large": LARGE_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH,
        },
    }


@app.post("/api/v1/embedding/small", response_model=EmbedddingResponse)
async def embedding_small(
    request: Request, payload: EmbeddingRequest
) -> EmbedddingResponse:
    """Generate embeddings using the small embedding model."""
    embeddings = await _encode_texts(request.app.state.embedder_small, payload.texts)
    return EmbedddingResponse(model=SMALL_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH, embeddings=embeddings)


@app.post("/api/v1/embedding/large", response_model=EmbedddingResponse)
async def embedding_large(
    request: Request, payload: EmbeddingRequest
) -> EmbedddingResponse:
    """Generate embeddings using the large embedding model."""
    embeddings = await _encode_texts(request.app.state.embedder_large, payload.texts)
    return EmbedddingResponse(model=LARGE_EMBEDDING_MODEL_PATH, embeddings=embeddings)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import uvicorn

    # TOOD - make changes wrt to Cloudera app environment
    uvicorn.run(
        "main:app",
        host="0.0.0.0",
        port=settings.embedding_api.embedding_service_port,
        access_log=False,
    )



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\api\rag_service\chat_pipeline.py
####################################################################################################

"""Request pipeline for the YONO Retail chatbot.

The pipeline validates the input, loads session history, checks the cache,
retrieves and reranks FAQ documents, generates an answer, validates the
output, persists the turn, and builds the API response. Each stage receives
the same :class:`ChatContext` instance and passes it to the next handler.

Notes
-----
Handlers skip work when ``context.resolved`` is true. This lets greetings,
blocked requests, and cache hits return early without running retrieval or
generation while still completing the response and persistence stages.
"""

import asyncio
import logging
import re
import secrets
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any

import httpx
from about_time import about_time

from api.utils import get_embedding_single_text, get_rerank_scores, normalize_query
from dlh_ai.config import get_settings
from dlh_ai.genai.agents import ChatAgent, build_context_items
from dlh_ai.genai.elasticsearch_ops import IST, ElasticsearchOps
from dlh_ai.genai.rag import (
    ChatRequest,
    ChatResponse,
    GuardrailViolationError,
    SearchHit,
)
from dlh_ai.genai.sentiment import SentimentService
from dlh_ai.models import (
    ChatCompletionResponse,
    ChatMessage,
    FaqVersionRef,
    LlmCacheResponse,
)

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from dlh_ai.genai.rag import GuardrailService

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")
settings = get_settings()

_GREETING_RE = re.compile(settings.chat_pipeline.greeting_pattern, re.IGNORECASE)


@dataclass
class ChatContext:
    """Mutable state threaded through the chat_completions pipeline."""

    session_id: str
    raw_message: str

    query: str = ""
    session: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    is_first_turn: bool = False

    cached: bool = False
    answer: str | None = None
    sources: list[FaqVersionRef] = field(default_factory=list)

    query_embedding: list[float] | None = None
    hits: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    top_hits: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    top_scores: list[float] = field(default_factory=list)
    history: list[dict[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)

    blocked_reason: str | None = None  # set by a guardrail check; the blocking exception's class name

    # True once `answer` has been decided by a short-circuit (input block, greeting
    # match, or cache hit) — tells every later handler to skip its own work rather
    # than redo it or overwrite the already-decided answer.
    resolved: bool = False

    response: ChatCompletionResponse | None = None


@dataclass
class ChatHandler(ABC):
    """One stage of chat_completions. Shared singleton across requests —
    process() may only mutate `ctx`, never `self`."""

    _next: "ChatHandler | None" = field(default=None, init=False, repr=False)

    def set_next(self, handler: "ChatHandler") -> "ChatHandler":
        self._next = handler
        return handler

    @abstractmethod
    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None: ...

    async def handle(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        logger.debug(
            f"chat_completions: session={ctx.session_id!r} running {type(self).__name__}"
        )
        with about_time() as t:  # type: ignore
            await self.process(ctx)
        logger.info(
            f"chat_completions: session={ctx.session_id!r} {type(self).__name__} "
            f"took {t.duration_human}"
        )
        if self._next is not None:
            await self._next.handle(ctx)


@dataclass
class InputGuardrailHandler(ChatHandler):
    guardrail_service: "GuardrailService | None"

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        if self.guardrail_service is None:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: input guardrails disabled")
            return
        if _GREETING_RE.match(ctx.raw_message):
            logger.debug("chat_completions: greeting detected, skipping input guardrails")
            return
        try:
            validated = self.guardrail_service.validate_input(
                ChatRequest(query=ctx.raw_message)
            )
        except GuardrailViolationError as exc:
            logger.warning(
                f"chat_completions: input blocked by guardrails "
                f"({type(exc).__name__}): {exc}"
            )
            ctx.blocked_reason = type(exc).__name__
            ctx.answer = str(exc)
            ctx.resolved = True
            return
        ctx.raw_message = validated.query


@dataclass
class SessionLookupHandler(ChatHandler):
    es_ops: ElasticsearchOps
    chat_agent: ChatAgent

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        ctx.query = normalize_query(ctx.raw_message)
        ctx.session = await self.es_ops.get_chat_session(ctx.session_id)
        ctx.is_first_turn = ctx.session is None or not ctx.session.get(
            "_source", {}
        ).get(self.es_ops.chat_messages_field)
        logger.debug(
            f"chat_completions: session={ctx.session_id!r} found={ctx.session is not None} "
            f"is_first_turn={ctx.is_first_turn}"
        )

        # Short-circuit with a static greeting when the customer just says hi
        # (skip if the input guardrail already blocked this message).
        if not ctx.resolved and _GREETING_RE.match(ctx.raw_message):
            ctx.answer = self.chat_agent.prompt_manager.get_prompt(
                "yono_retail_chatbot", "greeting"
            )
            ctx.resolved = True
            logger.info("chat_completions: greeting detected, returning static greeting")


@dataclass
class CacheLookupHandler(ChatHandler):
    es_ops: ElasticsearchOps
    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        if ctx.resolved or not (ctx.is_first_turn and settings.feature_flags.enable_llm_cache):
            logger.debug(
                "chat_completions: cache lookup skipped (already resolved, not first turn, "
                "or disabled by feature flag)"
            )
            return

        # Fan-out: exact-text cache lookup and embedding generation run in parallel
        # so the semantic lookup (which depends on the embedding) starts immediately
        # after both finish — saving one round-trip on cache misses.
        cache_hit, embedding = await asyncio.gather(
            self.es_ops.text_search_cache(ctx.query),
            get_embedding_single_text(self.http_client, ctx.query, model="small"),
        )

        if cache_hit is not None:
            logger.info("chat_completions: exact-text cache hit")
        else:
            semantic_hit = await self.es_ops.semantic_search_cache(embedding)
            top = semantic_hit["hits"]["hits"] if semantic_hit else []
            if top and top[0]["_score"] >= settings.chat_pipeline.cache_similarity_threshold:
                cache_hit = semantic_hit
                logger.info(
                    f"chat_completions: semantic cache hit, score={top[0]['_score']:.4f}"
                )

        if cache_hit is None:
            logger.info("chat_completions: cache miss")
            return

        top_hit = cache_hit["hits"]["hits"][0]
        hit_source = top_hit["_source"]
        cache_id = hit_source.get("cache_id") or top_hit["_id"]
        await self.es_ops.record_cache_hit(cache_id)
        ctx.cached = True
        ctx.resolved = True
        ctx.answer = hit_source["answer"]
        ctx.sources = [FaqVersionRef(**ref) for ref in hit_source["knowledge_snapshot"]]


@dataclass
class RetrievalHandler(ChatHandler):
    es_ops: ElasticsearchOps
    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        if ctx.resolved:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: retrieval skipped (already resolved)")
            return

        with about_time() as t:  # type: ignore
            ctx.query_embedding = await get_embedding_single_text(
                self.http_client, ctx.query, model="large"
            )
            search_result = await self.es_ops.hybrid_search_faq(
                settings.elastic.faq_index_name,
                ctx.query,
                ctx.query_embedding,
                k=settings.chat_pipeline.retrieve_k,
            )
        ctx.hits = search_result["hits"]["hits"]
        logger.info(
            f"chat_completions: retrieved {len(ctx.hits)} candidates in {t.duration_human}"
        )


@dataclass
class RerankHandler(ChatHandler):
    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        if ctx.resolved:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: rerank skipped (already resolved)")
            return

        documents = [hit["_source"]["combined"] for hit in ctx.hits]
        if documents and settings.feature_flags.enable_reranking:
            with about_time() as t:  # type: ignore
                rerank_scores = await get_rerank_scores(
                    self.http_client, ctx.query, documents
                )
            logger.info(
                f"chat_completions: reranked {len(documents)} candidates in {t.duration_human}"
            )
        else:
            rerank_scores = []
            logger.debug(
                "chat_completions: rerank disabled by feature flag or no candidates"
            )

        if rerank_scores:
            ranked = sorted(
                zip(ctx.hits, rerank_scores), key=lambda pair: pair[1], reverse=True
            )
        else:
            ranked = [(hit, hit.get("_score", 0.0)) for hit in ctx.hits]
        context_k = settings.chat_pipeline.context_k
        ctx.top_hits = [hit for hit, _ in ranked[:context_k]]
        ctx.top_scores = [score for _, score in ranked[:context_k]]


@dataclass
class HistoryHandler(ChatHandler):
    es_ops: ElasticsearchOps

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        if ctx.resolved:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: history skipped (already resolved)")
            return

        if not settings.feature_flags.enable_chat_history:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: history disabled by feature flag")
            ctx.history = []
            return

        ctx.history = self._build_history(ctx.session)
        logger.debug(f"chat_completions: including {len(ctx.history)} prior turns")

    def _build_history(self, session: dict | None) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        """Pair up the most recent (user, assistant) turns from a chat session for the prompt."""
        if session is None:
            return []
        messages = session.get("_source", {}).get(self.es_ops.chat_messages_field, [])
        user_turns = [m for m in messages if m["role"] == "user"]
        assistant_turns = [m for m in messages if m["role"] == "assistant"]
        turns = [
            {"query": u["text"], "answer": a["text"]}
            for u, a in zip(user_turns, assistant_turns)
        ]
        max_turns = settings.feature_flags.max_history_turns
        return turns[-max_turns:] if max_turns > 0 else turns


@dataclass
class GenerationHandler(ChatHandler):
    chat_agent: ChatAgent

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        if ctx.resolved:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: generation skipped (already resolved)")
            return

        # No retrieved hits → use fallback prompt (no LLM context injection).
        if not ctx.top_hits:
            logger.info("chat_completions: no hits retrieved, using fallback prompt")
            ctx.answer = await self.chat_agent.fallback(ctx.raw_message)
            return

        context_items = build_context_items(ctx.top_hits, ctx.top_scores)
        with about_time() as t:  # type: ignore
            ctx.answer = await self.chat_agent.answer(
                ctx.raw_message, context_items, ctx.history
            )
        logger.info(f"chat_completions: answer generated in {t.duration_human}")

        ctx.sources = [
            FaqVersionRef(
                faq_id=hit["_source"]["faq_id"], faq_version=hit["_source"]["faq_version"]
            )
            for hit in ctx.top_hits
        ]


def _to_search_hits(top_hits: list[dict[str, Any]], top_scores: list[float]) -> list[SearchHit]:
    """Adapt post-rerank ES hits to the shape GuardrailService validates against."""
    return [
        SearchHit(
            score=score,
            sno=0,
            doc_type="",
            category=hit["_source"].get("category", ""),
            question=hit["_source"].get("question", ""),
            answer=hit["_source"].get("answer", hit["_source"].get("combined", "")),
        )
        for hit, score in zip(top_hits, top_scores)
    ]


def _to_guardrail_response(answer: str | None, hits: list[SearchHit]) -> ChatResponse:
    top = hits[0] if hits else None
    return ChatResponse(
        query="",
        answer=answer or "",
        category=top.category if top else "",
        doc_type=top.doc_type if top else "",
        score=top.score if top else 0.0,
        sources=hits,
    )


@dataclass
class OutputGuardrailHandler(ChatHandler):
    guardrail_service: "GuardrailService | None"

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        if self.guardrail_service is None or ctx.resolved:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: output guardrails skipped")
            return

        hits = _to_search_hits(ctx.top_hits, ctx.top_scores)
        try:
            validated = self.guardrail_service.validate_output(
                _to_guardrail_response(ctx.answer, hits), hits
            )
        except GuardrailViolationError as exc:
            logger.warning(
                f"chat_completions: output blocked by guardrails "
                f"({type(exc).__name__}): {exc}"
            )
            ctx.blocked_reason = type(exc).__name__
            ctx.answer = str(exc)
            ctx.sources = []
            ctx.resolved = True
            return
        ctx.answer = validated.answer


@dataclass
class CacheWriteHandler(ChatHandler):
    es_ops: ElasticsearchOps

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        if ctx.resolved:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: cache write skipped (already resolved)")
            return
        if not settings.feature_flags.enable_llm_cache:
            logger.debug("chat_completions: cache write disabled by feature flag")
            return

        cache_id = secrets.token_hex(8)
        cache_response = LlmCacheResponse(
            cache_id=cache_id,
            normalized_question=ctx.query,
            query_embedding=ctx.query_embedding or [],
            knowledge_snapshot=ctx.sources,
            answer=ctx.answer or "",
            created_at=datetime.now(IST),
        )
        await self.es_ops.save_cache(cache_response.model_dump(mode="json"), cache_id)
        logger.info(f"chat_completions: wrote new cache entry {cache_id!r}")


@dataclass
class PersistTurnHandler(ChatHandler):
    es_ops: ElasticsearchOps
    sentiment_service: SentimentService

    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        await self._persist_turn(
            ctx.session_id, ctx.session, ctx.raw_message, ctx.answer or "", ctx.sources
        )
        logger.debug(f"chat_completions: session={ctx.session_id!r} turn persisted")

    async def _persist_turn(
        self,
        session_id: str,
        session: dict | None,
        user_text: str,
        answer_text: str,
        sources: list[FaqVersionRef],
    ) -> None:
        """Append the user + assistant messages for this turn to the chat session."""
        existing_count = (
            len(session.get("_source", {}).get(self.es_ops.chat_messages_field, []))
            if session
            else 0
        )
        sentiment = self.sentiment_service.analyze(user_text)
        now = datetime.now(IST)

        user_message = ChatMessage(
            id=existing_count + 1,
            role="user",
            text=user_text,
            retrieved_docs=[],
            timestamp=now,
            sentiment=sentiment.label,
        )
        assistant_message = ChatMessage(
            id=existing_count + 2,
            role="assistant",
            text=answer_text,
            retrieved_docs=sources,
            timestamp=now,
            sentiment=None,
        )
        await self.es_ops.add_chat_message(session_id, user_message)
        await self.es_ops.add_chat_message(session_id, assistant_message)


@dataclass
class ResponseBuilderHandler(ChatHandler):
    async def process(self, ctx: ChatContext) -> None:
        ctx.response = ChatCompletionResponse(
            session_id=ctx.session_id,
            answer=ctx.answer or "",
            cached=ctx.cached,
            sources=ctx.sources,
        )
        logger.debug(
            f"chat_completions: session={ctx.session_id!r} cached={ctx.cached} "
            f"sources={len(ctx.sources)}"
        )


def build_chat_pipeline(
    es_ops: ElasticsearchOps,
    chat_agent: ChatAgent,
    sentiment_service: SentimentService,
    http_client: httpx.AsyncClient,
    guardrail_service: "GuardrailService | None",
) -> ChatHandler:
    """Wire the chat_completions pipeline and return its head handler."""
    head = InputGuardrailHandler(guardrail_service)
    head.set_next(SessionLookupHandler(es_ops, chat_agent)).set_next(
        CacheLookupHandler(es_ops, http_client)
    ).set_next(RetrievalHandler(es_ops, http_client)).set_next(
        RerankHandler(http_client)
    ).set_next(HistoryHandler(es_ops)).set_next(GenerationHandler(chat_agent)).set_next(
        OutputGuardrailHandler(guardrail_service)
    ).set_next(CacheWriteHandler(es_ops)).set_next(
        PersistTurnHandler(es_ops, sentiment_service)
    ).set_next(ResponseBuilderHandler())
    return head

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Annotated

import httpx
from fastapi import Depends, Query, Request

from api.rag_service.chat_pipeline import ChatHandler
from api.utils import get_embedding_single_text, normalize_query
from dlh_ai.config import get_settings
from dlh_ai.genai.agents import ChatAgent
from dlh_ai.genai.elasticsearch_ops import ElasticsearchOps
from dlh_ai.genai.sentiment import SentimentService
from dlh_ai.models import VectorSearchRequest

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from dlh_ai.genai.rag import GuardrailService

settings = get_settings()


def get_normalized_query(query: Annotated[str, Query(min_length=1)]) -> str:
    """Dependency that reads ``query`` and returns its normalized form."""
    return normalize_query(query)


def get_vector_search_request(
    request: Annotated[VectorSearchRequest, Query()],
) -> VectorSearchRequest:
    """Parse vector search query params once per request."""
    return request


def get_http_client(request: Request) -> httpx.AsyncClient:
    """Return the pooled HTTP client created at application startup."""
    return request.app.state.http_client


def get_es_ops(request: Request) -> ElasticsearchOps:
    """Return the Elasticsearch operations client created at application startup."""
    return request.app.state.es_ops


def get_chat_agent(request: Request) -> ChatAgent:
    """Return the chat agent created at application startup."""
    return request.app.state.chat_agent


def get_sentiment_service(request: Request) -> SentimentService:
    """Return the sentiment service created at application startup."""
    return request.app.state.sentiment_service


def get_chat_pipeline(request: Request) -> ChatHandler:
    """Return the chat_completions pipeline's head handler, built at application startup."""
    return request.app.state.chat_pipeline


def get_guardrail_service(request: Request) -> "GuardrailService | None":
    """Return the guardrail service, or None if disabled via config."""
    return request.app.state.guardrail_service


async def get_faq_query_embedding(
    request: Annotated[VectorSearchRequest, Depends(get_vector_search_request)],
    http_client: Annotated[httpx.AsyncClient, Depends(get_http_client)],
) -> list[float]:
    """Generate an embedding for the query text via the embedding service."""
    return await get_embedding_single_text(http_client, request.query_text, model="large")


async def get_cache_query_embedding(
    request: Annotated[VectorSearchRequest, Depends(get_vector_search_request)],
    http_client: Annotated[httpx.AsyncClient, Depends(get_http_client)],
) -> list[float]:
    """Generate an embedding for the query text via the embedding service."""
    return await get_embedding_single_text(http_client, request.query_text, model="small")

import asyncio
import os
import http
import logging
import traceback
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Annotated

import httpx
from about_time import about_time
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request, status
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from scalar_fastapi import get_scalar_api_reference

from api.rag_service.chat_pipeline import ChatContext, ChatHandler, build_chat_pipeline
from api.rag_service.dependency import (
    get_chat_pipeline,
    get_es_ops,
    get_faq_query_embedding,
    get_http_client,
    get_normalized_query,
    get_sentiment_service,
    get_vector_search_request,
)
from api.utils import (
    check_elasticsearch_availability,
    check_embedding_service_health,
    check_llm_service_health,
    check_reranker_service_health,
    get_embedding_single_text,
)
from dlh_ai.config import get_settings
from dlh_ai.genai.agents import ChatAgent
from dlh_ai.genai.elasticsearch_ops import ElasticsearchOps
from dlh_ai.genai.prompt import PromptManager
from dlh_ai.genai.rag import LlmService
from dlh_ai.genai.sentiment import SentimentService
from dlh_ai.models import (
    ChatCompletionRequest,
    ChatCompletionResponse,
    SentimentAnalyzeRequest,
    SentimentAnalyzeResponse,
    VectorSearchRequest,
)

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from dlh_ai.genai.rag import GuardrailService

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")
settings = get_settings()

_uvicorn_server: uvicorn.Server | None = None
_uvicorn_task: asyncio.Task | None = None

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0) as http_client:
        app.state.http_client = http_client
        await check_embedding_service_health(http_client)
        await check_elasticsearch_availability()
        await check_llm_service_health(http_client)
        #await check_reranker_service_health(http_client)
        if settings.feature_flags.enable_reranking:
            await check_reranker_service_health(http_client)

        # Quoted deliberately: GuardrailService is only bound under
        # TYPE_CHECKING / the deferred import below, so an unquoted
        # annotation would raise NameError at runtime.
        guardrail_service: "GuardrailService | None" = None 
        if settings.feature_flags.enable_guardrails:
            from dlh_ai.genai.rag import (
                GuardrailService,  # deferred: only needed when the flag is on
            )

            guardrail_service = GuardrailService(
                pii_entities=settings.guardrails.pii_entities,
                enable_preprocessing=settings.guardrails.enable_preprocessing,
                enable_pii_redaction=settings.guardrails.enable_pii_redaction,
                enable_profanity_filter=settings.guardrails.enable_profanity_filter,
                grounding_threshold=settings.guardrails.grounding_threshold,
            )
        app.state.guardrail_service = guardrail_service

        es_ops = ElasticsearchOps(
            faq_index=settings.elastic.faq_index_name,
            cache_index=settings.elastic.llm_cache_index_name,
            chat_sessions_index=settings.elastic.chat_sessions_index_name,
        )
        app.state.es_ops = es_ops

        llm_service = LlmService(
            model_name=settings.common.llm_model_name,
            base_url=settings.common.llm_base_url,
            api_key=settings.common.llm_api_key,
            verify_ssl=settings.common.llm_verify_ssl,
            temperature=settings.common.llm_temperature,
            max_tokens=settings.common.llm_max_tokens,
            request_timeout=settings.common.llm_request_timeout,
        )
        llm_service.load()
        chat_agent = ChatAgent(llm_service=llm_service)
        app.state.chat_agent = chat_agent

        sentiment_service = SentimentService()
        app.state.sentiment_service = sentiment_service

        app.state.chat_pipeline = build_chat_pipeline(
            es_ops, chat_agent, sentiment_service, http_client, guardrail_service
        )

        yield
        await llm_service.shutdown()


app = FastAPI(
    debug=True,
    title="Yono Chatbot RAG Service",
    version="0.0.1",
    docs_url=None,
    redoc_url=None,
    lifespan=lifespan,
)


@app.middleware("http")
async def _log_request_information(request: Request, call_next):
    # Reconstruct the path and query string without sensitive data
    path_and_query_url = (
        f"{request.url.path}?{request.query_params}"
        if request.query_params
        else request.url.path
    )

    # tracking time taken by request to be fulfilled
    with about_time() as t:  # type: ignore
        # NOTE - Here the actual http function will be executed in the middleware.
        logger.info(f"received request --> {request.method} {path_and_query_url}")
        response = await call_next(request)
        logger.info(
            f"fulfilled request --> {request.method} {path_and_query_url} {response.status_code}"
            f" {http.HTTPStatus(response.status_code).phrase} in {t.duration_human}"
        )
        return response


@app.exception_handler(Exception)
async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception):
    errors = traceback.format_exception(exc)
    logger.error("".join(errors))
    try:
        prompt_manager: PromptManager = app.state.chat_agent.prompt_manager
        user_detail = prompt_manager.get_prompt("yono_retail_chatbot", "error")
    except (AttributeError, KeyError, ValueError):
        user_detail = str(exc)
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
        content={"error": exc.__class__.__name__, "detail": user_detail},
    )


@app.get("/docs", include_in_schema=False)
async def _internal_scalar_html():
    return get_scalar_api_reference(
        openapi_url=app.openapi_url,
        title=app.title,
    )


@app.get("/", status_code=200)
async def _index():
    """API Health check"""
    return {"message": "Yono Chatbot RAG Service is available"}




@app.get("/api/v1/search/vector")
async def search_vector(
    request: Annotated[VectorSearchRequest, Depends(get_vector_search_request)],
    embedding: Annotated[list[float], Depends(get_faq_query_embedding)],
    es_ops: Annotated[ElasticsearchOps, Depends(get_es_ops)],
):
    results = await es_ops.hybrid_search_faq(
        request.index, request.query_text, embedding, request.k, request.num_candidates
    )
    return results


@app.get("/api/v1/search/cache")
async def search_cache(
    query: Annotated[str, Depends(get_normalized_query)],
    es_ops: Annotated[ElasticsearchOps, Depends(get_es_ops)],
    http_client: Annotated[httpx.AsyncClient, Depends(get_http_client)],
):
    # First try to find an exact match in the cache
    results = await es_ops.text_search_cache(query, size=1)
    if results is not None:
        return results

    # If no exact match, try to find a semantic match in the cache
    embedding = await get_embedding_single_text(http_client, query)
    results = await es_ops.semantic_search_cache(embedding)
    if results is not None:
        return results

    return JSONResponse(
        content={"error": "No cache hit found"},
        status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    )




@app.post(
    "/api/v1/chat/completions", response_model=ChatCompletionResponse
)  # REVIEW - We can actually make it GET request & use query params instead of request body.
async def chat_completions(
    request: ChatCompletionRequest,
    pipeline: Annotated[ChatHandler, Depends(get_chat_pipeline)],
) -> ChatCompletionResponse:
    """Answer a chat message, serving from the LLM cache when possible.

    Runs the chat_completions pipeline (see `chat_pipeline.py`): session
    lookup, cache lookup, retrieval, rerank, history, generation, cache
    write, and turn persistence. Each stage is a Chain-of-Responsibility
    handler that no-ops when its feature flag is off or the request was
    already served from cache.
    """
    ctx = ChatContext(session_id=request.session_id, raw_message=request.message)
    await pipeline.handle(ctx)
    return ctx.response


@app.post("/api/v1/sentiment/analyze", response_model=SentimentAnalyzeResponse)
async def sentiment_analyze(
    payload: SentimentAnalyzeRequest,
    sentiment_service: Annotated[SentimentService, Depends(get_sentiment_service)],
) -> SentimentAnalyzeResponse:
    """Score a piece of text with the lexicon-based sentiment analyzer."""
    result = sentiment_service.analyze(payload.text)
    return SentimentAnalyzeResponse(label=result.label, score=result.score)

    except RuntimeError:
        # If this same module is ever executed as:
        # python your_service_file.py
        asyncio.run(_uvicorn_server.serve())
    else:

        _uvicorn_task = loop.create_task(_uvicorn_server.serve())

import asyncio
import http
import logging
import traceback
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import Annotated

from about_time import about_time
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request, status
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from scalar_fastapi import get_scalar_api_reference
from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder

from api.reranker_service.dependency import get_reranker
from dlh_ai.config import get_settings
from dlh_ai.models import RerankRequest, RerankResponse
from dlh_ai.utils import HDFSClient

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")
settings = get_settings()

RERANKER_MODEL_PATH = settings.reranker_api.reranker_service_model_path


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    hdfs_client = HDFSClient()

    logger.info(f"loading reranker model from {RERANKER_MODEL_PATH}")
    app.state.reranker = hdfs_client.load_model_cross_encoder(RERANKER_MODEL_PATH)

    yield
    # clean up the model after the lifespan is complete
    app.state.reranker = None
    logger.info("cleaned up the model after the lifespan is complete")


app = FastAPI(
    debug=True,
    title="Yono Chatbot Reranker Service",
    version="0.0.1",
    docs_url=None,
    redoc_url=None,
    lifespan=lifespan,
)


# Middleware to log incoming request & processing timing
@app.middleware("http")
async def _log_request_information(request: Request, call_next):
    # Reconstruct the path and query string without sensitive data
    path_and_query_url = (
        f"{request.url.path}?{request.query_params}"
        if request.query_params
        else request.url.path
    )

    # tracking time taken by request to be fulfilled
    with about_time() as t:  # type: ignore
        # NOTE - Here the actual http function will be executed in the middleware.
        logger.info(f"received request --> {request.method} {path_and_query_url}")
        response = await call_next(request)
        logger.info(
            f"fulfilled request --> {request.method} {path_and_query_url} {response.status_code}"
            f" {http.HTTPStatus(response.status_code).phrase} in {t.duration_human}"
        )
        return response


@app.exception_handler(Exception)
async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception):
    errors = traceback.format_exception(exc)
    logger.error("".join(errors))
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
        content={"error": exc.__class__.__name__, "detail": str(exc)},
    )


# Scalar interactive docs
@app.get("/docs", include_in_schema=False)
async def _internal_scalar_html():
    return get_scalar_api_reference(
        openapi_url=app.openapi_url,
        title=app.title,
    )


# REST API for health check
@app.get("/", status_code=200)
async def _index():
    """API Health check"""
    return {
        "message": "Yono Chatbot Reranker Service is up and running",
        "model": RERANKER_MODEL_PATH,
    }


@app.post("/api/v1/rerank", response_model=RerankResponse)
async def rerank(
    payload: RerankRequest,
    reranker: Annotated[CrossEncoder, Depends(get_reranker)],
) -> RerankResponse:
    """Score (query, document) pairs with the cross-encoder reranker model."""
    pairs = [(payload.query, document) for document in payload.documents]
    scores = await asyncio.to_thread(reranker.predict, pairs)
    return RerankResponse(model=RERANKER_MODEL_PATH, scores=scores.tolist())

        host="0.0.0.0",
        port=settings.reranker_api.reranker_service_port,
        access_log=False,
    )

import logging
import os
from logging import Formatter, getLogger
from rich.console import Console
from rich.logging import RichHandler

LOG_DIR = "logs"
LOG_FILENAME = "app.log"
LOG_FORMAT_CONSOLE = "%(message)s"
LOG_FORMAT_FILE = "%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(name)s | %(module)s:%(filename)s:%(lineno)d | %(funcName)s | %(message)s"
DATE_FORMAT = "%d-%b-%y %H:%M:%S"
LOG_LEVEL = logging.DEBUG


"""Configures and returns the logger for the dc_nbo library."""
logger = getLogger("dlh_ai")
logger.setLevel(LOG_LEVEL)

# --- Console Handler ---
console_handler = RichHandler(
    markup=True,
    show_path=False,
    console=Console(),
    locals_max_string=150,
)
console_handler.setFormatter(Formatter(LOG_FORMAT_CONSOLE, datefmt=DATE_FORMAT))
console_handler.flush()  # Ensure immediate output
logger.addHandler(console_handler)

# --- File Handler ---
os.makedirs(LOG_DIR, exist_ok=True)

file_handler = logging.FileHandler(
    filename=os.path.join(LOG_DIR, LOG_FILENAME),
    mode="a",  # Append mode
    encoding="utf8",
)
file_handler.setFormatter(Formatter(LOG_FORMAT_FILE))
logger.addHandler(file_handler)

from pathlib import Path

from pydantic import BaseModel
from pydantic_settings import (
    BaseSettings,
    PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
    SettingsConfigDict,
    TomlConfigSettingsSource,
)


class Common(BaseModel):
    project_id: str
    mlflow_exp: str
    elasticsearch_hosts: list[str]
    elasticsearch_api_key: str
    elasticsearch_verify_certs: bool = False
    elasticsearch_request_timeout: float = 10.0
    elasticsearch_max_retries: int = 3
    elasticsearch_retry_on_timeout: bool = True
    llm_base_url: str
    llm_api_key: str
    llm_model_name: str
    llm_verify_ssl: bool = True
    llm_temperature: float = 0.2
    llm_max_tokens: int = 1024
    llm_request_timeout: float = 60.0


class EmbeddingApiService(BaseModel):
    embedding_service_base_url: str
    embedding_service_port: int
    embedding_service_small_model_path: str
    embedding_service_large_model_path: str


class RAGApiService(BaseModel):
    rag_service_base_url: str
    rag_service_port: int


class RerankerApiService(BaseModel):
    reranker_service_base_url: str
    reranker_service_port: int
    reranker_service_model_path: str


class Elastic(BaseModel):
    host: str
    api_key: str
    verify_certs: bool
    faq_index_name: str
    chat_sessions_index_name: str
    llm_cache_index_name: str
    embedding_dimension: int
    csv_file_path: str
    batch_size: int
    mlflow_experiment_name: str
    embedding_service_url: str
    force_recreate_indices: bool


class FeatureFlags(BaseModel):
    """Boolean switches for optional `chat_completions` pipeline steps."""

    enable_reranking: bool = True
    enable_llm_cache: bool = True
    enable_chat_history: bool = True
    max_history_turns: int = 5
    enable_guardrails: bool = False


class ChatPipeline(BaseModel):
    """Tuning values for the chat retrieval and greeting pipeline."""

    cache_similarity_threshold: float = 0.85
    retrieve_k: int = 10
    context_k: int = 3
    greeting_pattern: str = (
        r"^\s*(?:hi|hey|hello|namaste|namaskar|good\s+"
        r"(?:morning|afternoon|evening|day)|howdy|greetings|sup|yo|start|help)\W*$"
    )


class Guardrails(BaseModel):
    """Config for the optional input/output `GuardrailService` checks."""

    pii_entities: list[str] = [
        "CREDIT_CARD",
        "IN_PAN",
        "IN_AADHAAR",
        "PHONE_NUMBER",
        "EMAIL_ADDRESS",
        "IBAN_CODE",
        "ACCOUNT_NUMBER",
    ]
    enable_preprocessing: bool = True
    enable_pii_redaction: bool = True
    enable_profanity_filter: bool = True
    # enable_toxicity_filter / toxicity_threshold / detoxify_checkpoint_path are
    # reserved for a future ML-based toxicity filter. GuardrailService is
    # heuristic/regex-only today (see ToxicContentError's docstring) and its
    # constructor does not accept these three - they are not read anywhere yet.
    enable_toxicity_filter: bool = False
    toxicity_threshold: float = 0.7
    grounding_threshold: float = 0.15
    detoxify_checkpoint_path: str = ""


# the Settings model
class Settings(BaseSettings):
    """
        Application settings loaded from constructor args, environment variables,
        .env, and TOML.

        Nested fields can be overridden from environment variables by using the
        `env_nested_delimiter="__"` separator.
    settings
        Example overrides:
        - COMMON__DUMMY1=value
        - COMMON__EXPERIMENT_NAME=value
        - DATA__SOURCE_TABLE=value
        - DATA__SPARK_EXECUTOR_MEMORY=value
        - ELASTIC__API_KEY=value

        For example, `DATA__SOURCE_TABLE=prod.table` overrides:
        settings.data.source_table
    """

    common: Common
    embedding_api: EmbeddingApiService
    rag_api: RAGApiService
    reranker_api: RerankerApiService
    security_api: SecurityApiService
    elastic: Elastic
    feature_flags: FeatureFlags = FeatureFlags()
    chat_pipeline: ChatPipeline = ChatPipeline()
    guardrails: Guardrails = Guardrails()

    # Use "__" to target nested fields from environment variables.
    # Example:
    #   COMMON__EXPERIMENT_NAME=my_run
    #   DATA__FEATURE_TABLE=my_feature_table
    #   ELASTIC__API_KEY=my_secret  (also loaded from .env)
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_nested_delimiter="__", env_file=".env")

    @classmethod
    def settings_customise_sources(
        cls,
        settings_cls: type[BaseSettings],
        init_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        env_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        dotenv_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
        file_secret_settings: PydanticBaseSettingsSource,
    ) -> tuple[PydanticBaseSettingsSource, ...]:
        return (
            env_settings,  # 1nd priority - environment variables like DATA__SOURCE_TABLE
            dotenv_settings,  # 2rd priority - .env file entries using the same nested format
            TomlConfigSettingsSource(
                settings_cls
            ),  # 3th priority - config.toml defaults
            init_settings,  # 4th priority - direct values passed to Settings(...)
        )

    def save_as_toml(self) -> None:
        """Save the current settings to a TOML file."""
        import toml

        _file = self.model_config.get("toml_file")
        with open(_file, "w") as f:
            toml.dump(self.model_dump(), f)


def ensure_config_env(config_path: Path | str | None = None) -> Path:
    """Ensure that the given config path is actually indeed present. Return resolved path"""
    if not config_path:
        # the default config path
        p = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config.toml"
    else:
        p = Path(config_path)

    if p.is_file():
        return p.resolve()
    else:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Config file not found at: {p}")


def get_settings(config_path: str | Path | None = None) -> Settings:
    """Get the application settings"""
    resolved = ensure_config_env(config_path)

    # set toml file path for Settings before instantiation so BaseSettings reads it
    Settings.model_config["toml_file"] = resolved.as_posix()

    return Settings()

from datetime import date, datetime

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


# Elasticsearch Models
class FaqVersionRef(BaseModel):
    """Reference to a specific version of an FAQ document."""

    faq_id: str
    faq_version: int


class FaqDocument(BaseModel):
    """FAQ document model"""

    faq_id: str
    faq_version: int
    category: str
    type: str
    question: str
    answer: str
    combined: str
    embedding: list[float]
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime


class ChatMessage(BaseModel):
    """Chat message model"""

    id: int
    role: str
    text: str
    retrieved_docs: list[FaqVersionRef] = Field(default_factory=list)
    timestamp: datetime


class ChatSession(BaseModel):
    session_id: str
    created_at: date
    updated_at: date
    message: list[ChatMessage] = Field(default_factory=list)


class LlmCacheResponse(BaseModel):
    cache_id: str
    normalized_question: str
    query_embedding: list[float]
    knowledge_snapshot: list[FaqVersionRef] = Field(default_factory=list)
    answer: str
    stale: bool = False
    cache_hit_count: int = 0
    created_at: datetime


# SECTION - API Service Models

    texts: list[str] = Field(..., min_length=1)


class EmbedddingResponse(BaseModel):
    """Embedding generation response."""

    model: str
    embeddings: list[list[float]]


# RAG Service Models

    """Request query parameters for vector search."""

    model_config = {"extra": "forbid"}

    index: str
    query_text: str
    k: int = 5
    num_candidates: int = 50

# Reranker Service Models

    """Request payload for cross-encoder reranking."""

    query: str
    documents: list[str] = Field(..., min_length=1)


class RerankResponse(BaseModel):
    """Reranking response."""

    model: str
    scores: list[float]

class ChatCompletionRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request payload for chat completion."""

    session_id: str
    message: str

####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\data\_pandas_backend.py
####################################################################################################

import logging
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

import cml.data_v1 as cmldata
import pandas as pd
from sqlglot import Expression, parse_one
from tenacity import (
    before_sleep_log,
    retry,
    retry_if_exception_type,
    stop_after_attempt,
    wait_exponential,
)

from dlh_ai.utils import HDFSClient

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")


@dataclass
class PandasEngine:
    connection_name: str = "DLH-DEV-UAT - Hive on Tez"

    def __post_init__(self):
        self.hdfs_client = HDFSClient()

    @property
    def cursor(self):
        """Property to get a database cursor for executing queries
        NOTE - This will create a new connection & manually it must be closed after use.
        """
        # TODO - check if this is needed or if we can just use self._create_connection.get_cursor()
        conn = self._create_connection()
        return conn.get_cursor()

    def read_table(self, query: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Read a Iceberg table on Hive OCP into a Pandas DataFrame using provided sql query

        Parameters
        ----------
        query : str
            SQL query to read the table

        Returns
        -------
        pd.DataFrame
            Pandas DataFrame containing the data from the specified SQL table
        """
        # verifying the read query
        self._verify_sql_query(query)

        with self._create_connection() as base_conn:
            with base_conn.cursor() as cursor:
                logger.debug("Executing query to read the table")
                from impala.util import as_pandas

                cursor.execute(query)
                data = as_pandas(cursor)
                logger.info(f"Successfully read {len(data)} rows")

        # NOTE - reading from 'Hive on Tez' also add table name as prefix to column names,
        # so we remove it here for consistency with other engines
        return data.rename(
            columns={col: col.upper().split(".")[-1] for col in data.columns}
        )

    def read_csv_from_hdfs(self, path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        data = self.hdfs_client.read_csv_file(path)
        return data.to_table().to_pandas(
            split_blocks=True,
            self_destruct=True,  # frees Arrow buffers after conversion
            zero_copy_only=False,
        )

    def read_parquet_from_hdfs(self, path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
        data = self.hdfs_client.read_parquet_table(path)
        return data.to_table().to_pandas(
            split_blocks=True,
            self_destruct=True,  # frees Arrow buffers after conversion
            zero_copy_only=False,
        )

    def save_table(
        self,
        df: pd.DataFrame,
        name: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
        batch_size: int = 5000,
    ):
        """Function to insert data from a Pandas DataFrame into a Iceberg table

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pd.DataFrame
            table data in the form of pandas dataframe
        name : str
            table name to write/append DEPRECATED -
        mode : Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"], optional
            how to write data, by default "append"
        """
        if mode != "append":
            raise NotImplementedError(
                f"Mode '{mode}' not yet implemented for Pandas Engine"
            )
        # Prepare Query with Backticks
        # NOTE - splitting by '.' to backtick the schema and table separately: `db`.`table`
        if "." in name:
            db, tbl = name.split(".")
            formatted_table_name = f"`{db}`.`{tbl}`"
        else:
            formatted_table_name = f"`{name}`"

        # Backtick every column name to be safe
        col_names = ", ".join([f"`{c.split('.')[-1]}`" for c in df.columns])
        placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(df.columns))

        insert_sql_query = (
            f"INSERT INTO {formatted_table_name} ({col_names}) VALUES ({placeholders})"
        )

        no_of_batches = self._calculate_batch_size(df, batch_size)
        logger.info(
            f"Starting to load data into {name} in batches of {batch_size} with total batches: {no_of_batches}"
        )

        with self._create_connection() as base_conn:
            with base_conn.cursor() as cursor:
                try:
                    # cursor loop
                    for idx, offset in enumerate(
                        range(0, len(df), batch_size), start=1
                    ):
                        batch = df.iloc[offset : offset + batch_size]
                        logger.debug(f"inserting batch {idx}: out of {no_of_batches}")
                        cursor.executemany(
                            insert_sql_query, batch.to_dict(orient="records")
                        )
                        logger.debug(
                            f"successfully inserted batch {idx} with {len(batch)} rows."
                        )

                    logger.info(f"Successfully loaded {len(df)} rows.")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"SQL Error: {insert_sql_query}")
                    logger.error(f"Details: {e}")
                    # FIXME - use precise exception chaining rather than generic. then use it below with `from e`
                    # raise from e
                    raise  # this is temporary until we fix the above

    def save_csv_to_hdfs(
        self,
        df: pd.DataFrame,
        path: str,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        """Save a pandas DataFrame as a CSV file to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pd.DataFrame
            The DataFrame to save.
        path : str
            The HDFS path where the CSV file will be saved.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to `pd.DataFrame.to_csv()`.
        """
        # REVIEW - Check if instead of local copy, we can directly use pyarrow table to write to hdfs
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            df.to_csv(f"{temp_dir}/data.csv", **kwargs)
            self.upload(f"{temp_dir}/data.csv", path)
        logger.info(f"saved pandas dataframe to HDFS at {path}")

    def save_parquet(self, df: pd.DataFrame, path: str, **kwargs):
        """Save a pandas DataFrame as a Parquet file to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pd.DataFrame
            The DataFrame to save.
        path : str
            The HDFS path where the Parquet file will be saved.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to `pd.DataFrame.to_parquet()`.
        """
        # REVIEW - Check if instead of local copy, we can directly use pyarrow table to write to hdfs
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            df.to_parquet(f"{temp_dir}/data.parquet", **kwargs)
            self.upload(f"{temp_dir}/data.parquet", path)
        logger.info(f"saved pandas dataframe to HDFS at {path}")

    @retry(
        stop=stop_after_attempt(5),
        wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, max=10),
        retry=retry_if_exception_type(Exception),
        reraise=True,
        before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
    )
    def _create_connection(self):
        """Attempt to create and return the DB base connection (with retries)."""
        # TODO - we should explore connection pooling or reuse to avoid the overhead of creating
        # a new connection for every operation, but for now we will create a new connection each
        # time and rely on retries to handle transient issues.

        # if not hasattr(self, "_base_conn") or self._base_conn is None:
        self._base_conn = cmldata.get_connection(self.connection_name)
        logger.info(f"Successfully established connection: {self.connection_name}")
        return self._base_conn.get_base_connection()

    def _calculate_batch_size(self, df: pd.DataFrame, batch_size) -> int:
        """Calculate total bactches based on batch size & data length."""
        # Simple heuristic: 5000 rows per batch as a starting point
        return len(df) // batch_size + (1 if len(df) % batch_size > 0 else 0)

    # TODO - Explore optimizing connection reuse or pooling using SQLAlchemy + Impala

    def _verify_sql_query(self, query: str) -> Expression:
        return parse_one(query)



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\data\_polars_backend.py
####################################################################################################

import logging
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

import cml.data_v1 as cmldata
import polars as pl
from sqlglot import Expression, parse_one
from tenacity import (
    before_sleep_log,
    retry,
    retry_if_exception_type,
    stop_after_attempt,
    wait_exponential,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")


@dataclass
class PolarsEngine:
    connection_name: str = "DLH-DEV-UAT - Hive on Tez"

    @property
    def cursor(self):
        """Property to get a database cursor for executing queries
        NOTE - This will create a new connection & manually it must be closed after use.
        """
        # TODO - check if this is needed or if we can just use self._create_connection.get_cursor()
        conn = self._create_connection()
        return conn.get_cursor()

    def read_table(
        self, query: str, schema_overrides: dict | None = None
    ) -> pl.DataFrame:
        """Read a SQL table into a Polars DataFrame using the provided connection and table name

        Parameters
        ----------
        name : str
            name of the SQL table to read, can include schema (e.g. "schema.table

        Returns
        -------
        pl.DataFrame
            Polars DataFrame containing the data from the specified SQL table
        """
        # verifying the read query
        self._verify_sql_query(query)

        with self._create_connection() as base_conn:
            with base_conn.cursor() as cursor:
                logger.debug("Executing query to read the table")
                data = pl.read_database(
                    query, connection=cursor, schema_overrides=schema_overrides
                )
                logger.info(f"Successfully read {len(data)} rows")

        # NOTE - reading from 'Hive on Tez' also add table name as prefix to column names,
        # so we remove it here for consistency with other engines
        return data.rename({col: col.upper().split(".")[-1] for col in data.columns})

    def read_csv(self, path: str) -> pl.DataFrame:
        # TODO - TO implement it use boto3 S3 route
        raise NotImplementedError(
            "Support for reading file format not yet implemented in Polars"
        )

    def read_parquet(self, path: str) -> pl.DataFrame:
        # TODO - TO implement it use boto3 S3 route
        raise NotImplementedError(
            "Support for reading file format not yet implemented in Polars"
        )

    def save_table(
        self,
        df: pl.DataFrame,
        name: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
        batch_size: int = 5000,
    ):
        """Function to insert data from a Polars DataFrame into a SQL table

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pl.DataFrame
            table data in the form of polars dataframe
        name : str
            table name to write/append DEPRECATED -
        mode : Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"], optional
            how to write data, by default "append"
        """
        if mode != "append":
            raise NotImplementedError(
                f"Mode '{mode}' not yet implemented for Polars Engine"
            )
        # Prepare Query with Backticks
        # NOTE - splitting by '.' to backtick the schema and table separately: `db`.`table`
        if "." in name:
            db, tbl = name.split(".")
            formatted_table_name = f"`{db}`.`{tbl}`"
        else:
            formatted_table_name = f"`{name}`"

        # Backtick every column name to be safe
        col_names = ", ".join([f"`{c.split('.')[-1]}`" for c in df.columns])
        placeholders = ", ".join(["%s"] * len(df.columns))

        insert_sql_query = (
            f"INSERT INTO {formatted_table_name} ({col_names}) VALUES ({placeholders})"
        )

        no_of_batches = self._calculate_batch_size(df, batch_size)
        logger.info(
            f"Starting to load data into {name} in batches of {batch_size} with total batches: {no_of_batches}"
        )

        with self._create_connection() as base_conn:
            with base_conn.cursor() as cursor:
                try:
                    # cursor loop
                    for idx, offset in enumerate(
                        range(0, len(df), batch_size), start=1
                    ):
                        batch = df.slice(offset, batch_size)
                        logger.debug(f"inserting batch {idx}: out of {no_of_batches}")
                        cursor.executemany(insert_sql_query, batch.to_dicts())
                        logger.debug(
                            f"successfully inserted batch {idx} with {len(batch)} rows."
                        )

                    logger.info(f"Successfully loaded {len(df)} rows.")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"SQL Error: {insert_sql_query}")
                    logger.error(f"Details: {e}")
                    # FIXME - use precise exception chaining rather than generic. then use it below with `from e`
                    # raise from e
                    raise  # this is temporary until we fix the above

    def save_csv_to_hdfs(
        self,
        df: pl.DataFrame,
        path: str,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        """Save a Polars DataFrame as a CSV file to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pl.DataFrame
            The DataFrame to save.
        path : str
            The HDFS path where the CSV file will be saved.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to `pd.DataFrame.to_csv()`.
        """
        # REVIEW - Check if instead of local copy, we can directly use pyarrow table to write to hdfs
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            df.write_csv(f"{temp_dir}/data.csv", **kwargs)
            self.upload(f"{temp_dir}/data.csv", path)
        logger.info(f"saved polars dataframe to HDFS at {path}")

    def save_parquet_to_hdfs(self, df: pl.DataFrame, path: str, **kwargs):
        """Save a Polars DataFrame as a Parquet file to HDFS.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : pl.DataFrame
            The DataFrame to save.
        path : str
            The HDFS path where the Parquet file will be saved.
        **kwargs : dict
            Additional keyword arguments to pass to `pl.DataFrame.to_parquet()`.
        """
        # REVIEW - Check if instead of local copy, we can directly use pyarrow table to write to hdfs
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            df.write_parquet(f"{temp_dir}/data.parquet", **kwargs)
            self.upload(f"{temp_dir}/data.parquet", path)
        logger.info(f"saved polars dataframe to HDFS at {path}")

    @retry(
        stop=stop_after_attempt(5),
        wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, max=10),
        retry=retry_if_exception_type(Exception),
        reraise=True,
        before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
    )
    def _create_connection(self):
        """Attempt to create and return the DB base connection (with retries)."""
        # TODO - we should explore connection pooling or reuse to avoid the overhead of creating
        # a new connection for every operation, but for now we will create a new connection each
        # time and rely on retries to handle transient issues.

        # if not hasattr(self, "_base_conn") or self._base_conn is None:
        self._base_conn = cmldata.get_connection(self.connection_name)
        logger.info(f"Successfully established connection: {self.connection_name}")
        return self._base_conn.get_base_connection()

    def _calculate_batch_size(self, df: pl.DataFrame, batch_size) -> int:
        """Calculate total bactches based on batch size & data length."""
        # Simple heuristic: 5000 rows per batch as a starting point
        return len(df) // batch_size + (1 if len(df) % batch_size > 0 else 0)

    # TODO - Explore optimizing connection reuse or pooling using SQLAlchemy + Impala

    def _verify_sql_query(self, query: str) -> Expression:
        return parse_one(query)



####################################################################################################
FILE: src\dlh_ai\data\_spark_backend.py
####################################################################################################

import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Literal
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from pyspark.sql import SparkSession
from pyspark.sql.dataframe import DataFrame
from sqlglot import parse_one

logger = logging.getLogger("dlh_ai")


@dataclass
class SparkEngine:
    """Spark-backed data access and persistence engine.

    Parameters
    ----------
    app_name_prefix : str
        Prefix used while constructing the Spark application name during
        session initialization.
    spark: SparkSession
        Provide already created spark session so that it will be used instead of creating a new one
    """

    app_name_prefix: str | None = None
    spark: SparkSession | None = None

    def __post_init__(self):
        """Initialize the Spark session for the engine instance.

        Notes
        -----
        The Spark application name is created by combining
        ``app_name_prefix`` with the current timestamp in the
        ``Asia/Kolkata`` timezone.
        """
        # FIXME - Below logic of creating spark session logic will work with
        # Notebook session because it can access default-spark.conf but not
        # with CML Jobs
        if self.spark:
            logger.debug(
                f"using existing spark session: {self.spark.sparkContext.appName}"
            )
        else:
            if self.app_name_prefix is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    " No spark session provided neither app name for creating a new one"
                )
            logger.debug("creating new spark session")
            sess_name = f"{self.app_name_prefix}-{datetime.now(ZoneInfo('Asia/Kolkata')).isoformat()}"
            self.spark = SparkSession.builder.appName(sess_name).getOrCreate()
            logger.debug(f"successfully created new spark session: {sess_name}")

    def read_table(self, name: str) -> DataFrame:
        """Read a table from the Spark catalog.

        Parameters
        ----------
        name : str
            Fully qualified table name to read.

        Returns
        -------
        DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame loaded from the requested table.
        """
        return self.spark.read.table(name)

    def read_csv(self, path: str) -> DataFrame:
        """Read a CSV dataset from the provided path.

        Parameters
        ----------
        path : str
            Filesystem/Ozone path from which the CSV data should be loaded.

        Returns
        -------
        DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame created from the CSV input.
        """
        return self.spark.read.format("csv").option("path", path).load()

    def read_parquet(self, path: str) -> DataFrame:
        """Read a Parquet dataset from the provided path.

        Parameters
        ----------
        path : str
            Filesystem/Ozone path from which the Parquet data should be loaded.

        Returns
        -------
        DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame created from the Parquet input.
        """
        return self.spark.read.format("parquet").option("path", path).load()

    def save_table(
        self,
        df: DataFrame,
        name: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
    ):
        """Persist a DataFrame as an Iceberg table.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame to be written.
        name : str
            Target table name for persistence.
        mode : {"overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"}, default="append"
            Write mode to use while saving the table.
        """
        df.write.mode(mode).format("iceberg").saveAsTable(name)

    def merge_table(self, df: DataFrame, merge_query: str):
        """Merge a DataFrame into a target Iceberg table using a SQL MERGE statement.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : DataFrame
            Source Spark DataFrame that will be exposed as a temporary view for
            the merge operation.
        merge_query : str
            SQL MERGE statement used to perform the upsert or merge logic.
        """
        # parsing merge query to validate syntax & extract source name for temp view
        parsed_query = parse_one(merge_query)
        source: str = parsed_query.args.get("using").name

        # creating temp view with source table name provided in merge query
        df.createOrReplaceTempView(source)
        logger.debug(f"created {source} temp view")

        self.spark.sql(merge_query)
        logger.info(
            f"successfully merged into {parsed_query.this.db}.{parsed_query.this.name}"
        )

    def save_csv(
        self,
        df: DataFrame,
        path: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
    ):
        """Write a DataFrame to CSV format.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame to be written.
        path : str
            Destination filesystem path for the CSV output.
        mode : {"overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"}, default="append"
            Write mode to use while saving the CSV data.
        """
        df.write.mode(mode).format("csv").save(path)

    def save_parquet(
        self,
        df: DataFrame,
        path: str,
        mode: Literal["overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"] = "append",
    ):
        """Write a DataFrame to Parquet format.

        Parameters
        ----------
        df : DataFrame
            Spark DataFrame to be written.
        path : str
            Destination filesystem path for the Parquet output.
        mode : {"overwrite", "append", "ignore", "error"}, default="append"
            Write mode to use while saving the Parquet data.
        """
        df.write.mode(mode).format("parquet").save(path)

    def close_spark_session(self):
        """Closes curent active spark session"""
        self.spark.stop()
